M11 · OpenAI Codex · 902bd9e06b3e

指令执行：从 /goal 到回执

一个内置指令如何被解析、路由、执行并写回会话？

第 11 / 11 章8 条源码 findingmain
本章路线问题→比喻→多条源码证据→四问→源码目录→交互图→逐行翻译
先用一个生活比喻

指令像机场值机单：先认出航班，再检查登机规则，交给正确柜台执行，最后把登机和到达状态写回系统。

先回答本章问题

一个内置指令如何被解析、路由、执行并写回会话？ 一轮任务可以问模型很多次，但每一次“想一想并行动”的小步都先拍一张现场快照，避免工具清单和提示词在同一步里前后不一致。

本章证据板：8 条 finding

不要把一段代码当成整个系统；下面每张卡分别说明事实、白话、工程影响、边界和可点击的固定源码。

01
L1factarchitecture

01 · 每个 turn 由多个 step 组成，step 内共享一次不可漂移的上下文快照

源码事实

run_turn 在采样前处理压缩、skills/plugins、session/input hooks 和待处理输入；随后为一个 step 捕获 StepContext，并用同一视图构造上下文、工具与模型请求。

白话解释

一轮任务可以问模型很多次，但每一次“想一想并行动”的小步都先拍一张现场快照，避免工具清单和提示词在同一步里前后不一致。

对自研 Harness 的含义

配置热更新只能在安全边界生效，换来 prompt cache 和工具调用的确定性。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 153–212
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 213–274
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 3418498f0142 · 打开账本提交 ↗
codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs:153–274固定提交 ↗
  153  pub(crate) async fn run_turn(
  154      sess: Arc<Session>,
  155      turn_context: Arc<TurnContext>,
  156      turn_extension_data: Arc<codex_extension_api::ExtensionData>,
  157      input: Vec<TurnInput>,
  158      prewarmed_client_session: Option<ModelClientSession>,
  159      cancellation_token: CancellationToken,
  160  ) -> CodexResult<Option<String>> {
  161      let mut client_session =
  162          prewarmed_client_session.unwrap_or_else(|| sess.services.model_client.new_session());
  163      // TODO(ccunningham): Pre-turn compaction runs before context updates and the
  164      // new user message are recorded. Estimate pending incoming items (context
  165      // diffs/full reinjection + user input) and trigger compaction preemptively
  166      // when they would push the thread over the compaction threshold.
  167      if let Err(err) = run_pre_sampling_compact(
  168          &sess,
  169          &turn_context,
  170          &mut client_session,
  171          &cancellation_token,
  172      )
  173      .await
  174      {
  175          if matches!(err.details(), CodexErrorDetails::TurnAborted) {
  176              run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await;
  177              return Err(err);
  178          }
  179          let error = err.to_codex_protocol_error();
  180          sess.emit_turn_error_lifecycle(turn_context.as_ref(), error.clone())
  181              .await;
  182          error!("Failed to run pre-sampling compact");
  183          return Ok(None);
  184      }
  185
  186      // run_turn owns the step used to seed context and make the first sampling request.
  187      let first_step_context = match sess
  188          .capture_step_context(Arc::clone(&turn_context), &cancellation_token)
  189          .await
  190      {
  191          Ok(step_context) => step_context,
  192          Err(err) if matches!(err.details(), CodexErrorDetails::TurnAborted) => {
  193              run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await;
  194              return Err(err);
  195          }
  196          Err(err) => return Err(err),
  197      };
  198      // Keep the exact model-visible state used by this turn and its inline compactions.
  199      let (mut world_state, display_roots) = tokio::join!(
  200          sess.record_context_updates_and_set_reference_context_item(first_step_context.as_ref()),
  201          turn_diff_display_roots(first_step_context.as_ref()),
  202      );
  203
  204      let Some((injection_items, explicitly_enabled_connectors)) = build_skills_and_plugins(
  205          &sess,
  206          first_step_context.as_ref(),
  207          &input,
  208          &cancellation_token,
  209      )
  210      .await
  211      else {
  212          return Ok(None);
  213      };
  214
  215      if run_pending_session_start_hooks(&sess, &turn_context).await {
  216          return Ok(None);
  217      }
  218      let mut can_drain_pending_input = input.is_empty();
  219      if run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await {
  220          return Ok(None);
  221      }
  222
  223      sess.merge_connector_selection(explicitly_enabled_connectors.clone())
  224          .await;
  225      sess.set_previous_turn_settings(Some(PreviousTurnSettings {
  226          model: turn_context.model_info.slug.clone(),
  227          comp_hash: turn_context.model_info.comp_hash.clone(),
  228          realtime_active: Some(turn_context.realtime_active),
  229      }))
  230      .await;
  231      for response_item in injection_items {
  232          sess.record_conversation_items(&turn_context, std::slice::from_ref(&response_item))
  233              .await;
  234      }
  235
  236      track_turn_resolved_config_analytics(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await;
  237
  238      let mut last_agent_message: Option<String> = None;
  239      let mut stop_hook_active = false;
  240      // Although from the perspective of codex.rs, TurnDiffTracker has the lifecycle of a Task which contains
  241      // many turns, from the perspective of the user, it is a single turn.
  242      let turn_diff_tracker = Arc::new(tokio::sync::Mutex::new(
  243          TurnDiffTracker::with_environment_display_roots(display_roots),
  244      ));
  245
  246      // `ModelClientSession` is turn-scoped and caches WebSocket + sticky routing state, so we reuse
  247      // one instance across retries within this turn.
  248      // Pending input is drained into history before building the next model request.
  249      // However, we defer that drain until after sampling in two cases:
  250      // 1. At the start of a turn, so the fresh turn input in `input` gets sampled first.
  251      // 2. After auto-compact, when model/tool continuation needs to resume before any steer.
  252
  253      let mut next_step_context = Some(first_step_context);
  254      loop {
  255          // Note that pending_input would be something like a message the user
  256          // submitted through the UI while the model was running. Though the UI
  257          // may support this, the model might not.
  258          let pending_input = if can_drain_pending_input {
  259              sess.input_queue.get_pending_input(&sess.active_turn).await
  260          } else {
  261              Vec::new()
  262          };
  263
  264          if run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &pending_input).await {
  265              break;
  266          }
  267
  268          let window_id = sess.current_window_id().await;
  269          super::rollout_budget::maybe_record_reminder(
  270              sess.as_ref(),
  271              turn_context.as_ref(),
  272              &window_id,
  273          )
  274          .await;
  275
  276          // Capture once so context, advertised tools, and tool calls share one request view.
  277          let step_context = match next_step_context.take() {
  278              Some(step_context) => step_context,
  279              None => {
  280                  sess.capture_step_context(Arc::clone(&turn_context), &cancellation_token)
  281                      .await?
  282              }
  283          };
  284          let sampling_request_result: CodexResult<_> = async {
  285              super::time_reminder::maybe_record_current_time_reminder(
  286                  sess.as_ref(),
  287                  turn_context.as_ref(),
  288                  &window_id,
  289              )
  290              .await?;
  291
  292              world_state = sess
  293                  .record_step_world_state_if_changed(&world_state, step_context.as_ref())
  294                  .await;
  295
  296              // Construct the input that we will send to the model.
  297              let sampling_request_input: Vec<ResponseItem> = async {
  298                  sess.clone_history()
  299                      .await
  300                      .for_prompt(&turn_context.model_info.input_modalities)
  301              }
  302              .instrument(trace_span!("run_turn.prepare_sampling_request_input"))
  303              .await;
  304
  305              let responses_metadata = turn_context.turn_metadata_state.to_responses_metadata(
  306                  sess.installation_id.clone(),
  307                  window_id,
  308                  CodexResponsesRequestKind::Turn,
  309              );
  310              run_sampling_request(
  311                  Arc::clone(&sess),
  312                  Arc::clone(&step_context),
  313                  Arc::clone(&turn_extension_data),
  314                  Arc::clone(&turn_diff_tracker),
  315                  &mut client_session,
  316                  &responses_metadata,
  317                  sampling_request_input,
  318                  cancellation_token.child_token(),
  319              )
  320              .await
  321          }
  322          .await;
  323          match sampling_request_result {
  324              Ok((sampling_request_output, sampling_request_input)) => {
  325                  let SamplingRequestResult {
  326                      needs_follow_up: model_needs_follow_up,
  327                      last_agent_message: sampling_request_last_agent_message,
  328                  } = sampling_request_output;
  329                  if model_needs_follow_up {
  330                      sess.input_queue
  331                          .accept_mailbox_delivery_for_current_turn(
  332                              &sess.active_turn,
  333                              &turn_context.sub_id,
  334                          )
  335                          .await;
  336                  }
  337                  can_drain_pending_input = true;
  338                  let (has_pending_input, token_status) = async {
  339                      let has_pending_input =
  340                          sess.input_queue.has_pending_input(&sess.active_turn).await;
  341                      let token_status = super::context_window::context_window_token_status(
  342                          sess.as_ref(),
  343                          turn_context.as_ref(),
  344                      )
  345                      .await;

  153  pub(crate) async fn run_turn(
  154      sess: Arc<Session>,
  155      turn_context: Arc<TurnContext>,
  156      input: Vec<TurnInput>,
  157      prewarmed_client_session: Option<ModelClientSession>,
  158      cancellation_token: CancellationToken,
  159  ) -> CodexResult<Option<String>> {
  160      // Record results from hooks that finished after the previous turn before this turn's user prompt.
  161      drain_async_hook_results(&sess, &turn_context, /*before_user_prompt*/ true).await;
  162
  163      let mut client_session =
  164          prewarmed_client_session.unwrap_or_else(|| sess.services.model_client.new_session());
  165      // TODO(ccunningham): Pre-turn compaction runs before context updates and the
  166      // new user message are recorded. Estimate pending incoming items (context
  167      // diffs/full reinjection + user input) and trigger compaction preemptively
  168      // when they would push the thread over the compaction threshold.
  169      if let Err(err) = run_pre_sampling_compact(
  170          &sess,
  171          &turn_context,
  172          &mut client_session,
  173          &cancellation_token,
  174      )
  175      .await
  176      {
  177          if matches!(err.details(), CodexErrorDetails::TurnAborted) {
  178              run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input, PersistContext::Standard)
  179                  .await;
  180              return Err(err);
  181          }
  182          if matches!(err.details(), CodexErrorDetails::ToolCollision(_)) {
  183              return Err(err);
  184          }
  185          let error = err.to_codex_protocol_error();
  186          sess.emit_turn_error_lifecycle(turn_context.as_ref(), error.clone())
  187              .await;
  188          error!("Failed to run pre-sampling compact");
  189          return Ok(None);
  190      }
  191
  192      let user_input = turn_user_input(&input);
  193      let (required_servers, mentioned_plugins) =
  194          match required_mcp_servers_for_input(&sess, turn_context.as_ref(), &user_input)
  195              .or_cancel(&cancellation_token)
  196              .await
  197          {
  198              Ok(requirements) => requirements,
  199              Err(err) => {
  200                  run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input, PersistContext::Standard)
  201                      .await;
  202                  return Err(err.into());
  203              }
  204          };
  205
  206      // run_turn owns the step used to seed context and make the first sampling request.
  207      let first_step_context = match sess
  208          .capture_step_context_with_required_mcp_servers(
  209              Arc::clone(&turn_context),
  210              &cancellation_token,
  211              &required_servers,
  212          )
  213          .await
  214      {
  215          Ok(step_context) => step_context,
  216          Err(err) if matches!(err.details(), CodexErrorDetails::TurnAborted) => {
  217              run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input, PersistContext::Standard)
  218                  .await;
  219              return Err(err);
  220          }
  221          Err(err) => return Err(err),
  222      };
  223      // Keep the exact model-visible state used by this turn and its inline compactions.
  224      let (world_state, display_roots) = tokio::join!(
  225          sess.record_context_updates_and_set_reference_context_item(first_step_context.as_ref()),
  226          turn_diff_display_roots(first_step_context.as_ref()),
  227      );
  228      let mut world_state = world_state?;
  229
  230      let Some((injection_items, explicitly_enabled_connectors)) = build_skills_and_plugins(
  231          &sess,
  232          first_step_context.as_ref(),
  233          &user_input,
  234          &mentioned_plugins,
  235          &cancellation_token,
  236      )
  237      .await
  238      else {
  239          return Ok(None);
  240      };
  241
  242      if run_pending_session_start_hooks(&sess, &turn_context).await {
  243          return Ok(None);
  244      }
  245      let mut can_drain_pending_input = input.is_empty();
  246      if run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input, PersistContext::TurnStart).await {
  247          return Ok(None);
  248      }
  249
  250      sess.merge_connector_selection(explicitly_enabled_connectors.clone())
  251          .await;
  252      sess.set_previous_turn_settings(Some(PreviousTurnSettings {
  253          model: turn_context.model_info.slug.clone(),
  254          comp_hash: turn_context.model_info.comp_hash.clone(),
  255          realtime_active: Some(turn_context.realtime_active),
  256      }))
  257      .await;
  258      for response_item in injection_items {
  259          sess.record_conversation_items(&turn_context, std::slice::from_ref(&response_item))
  260              .await;
  261      }
  262
  263      track_turn_resolved_config_analytics(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await;
  264
  265      let mut last_agent_message: Option<String> = None;
  266      let mut stop_hook_active = false;
  267      // Although from the perspective of codex.rs, TurnDiffTracker has the lifecycle of a Task which contains
  268      // many turns, from the perspective of the user, it is a single turn.
  269      let turn_diff_tracker = Arc::new(tokio::sync::Mutex::new(
  270          TurnDiffTracker::with_environment_display_roots(display_roots),
  271      ));
  272
  273      // `ModelClientSession` is turn-scoped and caches WebSocket + sticky routing state, so we reuse
  274      // one instance across retries within this turn.
  275      // Pending input is drained into history before building the next model request.
  276      // However, we defer that drain until after sampling in two cases:
  277      // 1. At the start of a turn, so the fresh turn input in `input` gets sampled first.
  278      // 2. After auto-compact, when model/tool continuation needs to resume before any steer.
  279
  280      let mut next_step_context = Some(first_step_context);
  281      loop {
  282          // Note that pending_input would be something like a message the user
  283          // submitted through the UI while the model was running. Though the UI
  284          // may support this, the model might not.
  285          let pending_input = if can_drain_pending_input {
  286              sess.input_queue
  287                  .get_pending_input(&sess.active_turn)
  288                  .await
  289                  .0
  290          } else {
  291              Vec::new()
  292          };
  293
  294          if run_hooks_and_record_inputs(
  295              &sess,
  296              &turn_context,
  297              &pending_input,
  298              PersistContext::Standard,
  299          )
  300          .await
  301          {
  302              break;
  303          }
  304
  305          let window_id = sess.current_window_id().await;
  306          super::rollout_budget::maybe_record_reminder(
  307              sess.as_ref(),
  308              turn_context.as_ref(),
  309              &window_id,
  310          )
  311          .await;
  312
  313          // Capture once so context, advertised tools, and tool calls share one request view.
  314          let step_context = match next_step_context.take() {
  315              Some(step_context) => step_context,
  316              None if pending_input.is_empty() => {
  317                  sess.capture_step_context(Arc::clone(&turn_context), &cancellation_token)
  318                      .await?
  319              }
  320              None => {
  321                  let pending_user_input = turn_user_input(&pending_input);
  322                  let (required_servers, _) = required_mcp_servers_for_input(
  323                      &sess,
  324                      turn_context.as_ref(),
  325                      &pending_user_input,
  326                  )
  327                  .or_cancel(&cancellation_token)
  328                  .await?;
  329                  sess.capture_step_context_with_required_mcp_servers(
  330                      Arc::clone(&turn_context),
  331                      &cancellation_token,
  332                      &required_servers,
  333                  )
  334                  .await?
  335              }
  336          };
  337          let sampling_request_result: CodexResult<_> = async {
  338              super::time_reminder::maybe_record_current_time_reminder(
  339                  sess.as_ref(),
  340                  turn_context.as_ref(),
  341                  &window_id,
  342              )
  343              .await?;
  344
  345              world_state = sess
02
L1factarchitecture

02 · 流式采样与工具 Future 同时推进，并可被新消息抢占

源码事实

try_run_sampling_request 消费流式 ResponseEvent；完整 tool item 到达后进入 FuturesOrdered，工具参数 diff 可边流边展示；若 commentary/reasoning 期间 mailbox 有消息，则返回 needs_follow_up。

白话解释

模型说到一个完整动作就能开工，不必等整段回答结束；用户中途补充信息时，系统也能在安全位置收住并接新指令。

对自研 Harness 的含义

响应更快，但对调用顺序、取消、配对和幂等要求很高。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 2034–2100
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 2101–2168
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

03
L1factarchitecture

03 · 重试预算属于 turn-scoped client session，窗口超限不当作普通网络错误重试

源码事实

run_sampling_request 在一个 turn 内复用 ModelClientSession；上下文超限和 usage limit 直接上抛，其他可重试错误才按 Provider 的 stream retry 上限重试。

白话解释

同一轮尽量复用连接和粘性状态；行李箱塞不下不会盲目重拨网络，而是交给压缩逻辑处理。

对自研 Harness 的含义

把语义恢复与传输恢复分开，避免无效重试放大费用。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1176–1223
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 1224–1273
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 3418498f0142 · 打开账本提交 ↗
codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs:1176–1273固定提交 ↗
 1176  async fn run_sampling_request(
 1177      sess: Arc<Session>,
 1178      step_context: Arc<StepContext>,

 1183      input: Vec<ResponseItem>,
 1184      cancellation_token: CancellationToken,
 1185  ) -> CodexResult<(SamplingRequestResult, Vec<ResponseItem>)> {

 1187      let router = Arc::clone(&step_context.tool_router);
 1188
 1189      let base_instructions = sess.get_base_instructions().await;
 1190
 1191      let tool_runtime = ToolCallRuntime::new(

 1194          Arc::clone(&step_context),
 1195          Arc::clone(&turn_diff_tracker),
 1196      );
 1197      let _code_mode_worker = sess.services.code_mode_service.start_turn_worker(
 1198          &sess,

 1200          Arc::clone(&router),
 1201          Arc::clone(&turn_diff_tracker),
 1202      );
 1203      let max_retries = turn_context.provider.info().stream_max_retries();
 1204      let mut retries = 0;
 1205      let mut initial_input = Some(input);
 1206      let mut original_input = None;
 1207      loop {
 1208          let prompt_input = if let Some(input) = initial_input.take() {
 1209              input
 1210          } else {
 1211              sess.clone_history()
 1212                  .await
 1213                  .for_prompt(&turn_context.model_info.input_modalities)
 1214          };
 1215          let prompt = build_prompt(
 1216              prompt_input,
 1217              router.as_ref(),
 1218              turn_context.as_ref(),
 1219              base_instructions.clone(),
 1220          );
 1221          let err = match try_run_sampling_request(
 1222              tool_runtime.clone(),
 1223              Arc::clone(&sess),
 1224              Arc::clone(&turn_context),
 1225              Arc::clone(&turn_store),
 1226              client_session,
 1227              responses_metadata,
 1228              Arc::clone(&turn_diff_tracker),
 1229              &prompt,
 1230              cancellation_token.child_token(),
 1231          )
 1232          .await
 1233          {
 1234              Ok(output) => {
 1235                  return Ok((output, original_input.unwrap_or(prompt.input)));
 1236              }
 1237              Err(err) => match err.details() {
 1238                  CodexErrorDetails::ContextWindowExceeded => {
 1239                      sess.set_total_tokens_full(&turn_context).await;
 1240                      return Err(err);
 1241                  }
 1242                  CodexErrorDetails::UsageLimitReached(e) => {
 1243                      let rate_limits = e.rate_limits.clone();
 1244                      if let Some(rate_limits) = rate_limits {
 1245                          sess.update_rate_limits(&turn_context, *rate_limits).await;
 1246                      }
 1247                      return Err(err);
 1248                  }
 1249                  _ => err,
 1250              },
 1251          };
 1252
 1253          if original_input.is_none() {
 1254              original_input = Some(prompt.input);
 1255          }
 1256
 1257          if !err.is_retryable() {
 1258              return Err(err);
 1259          }
 1260
 1261          handle_retryable_response_stream_error(
 1262              &mut retries,
 1263              max_retries,
 1264              err,
 1265              client_session,
 1266              &sess,
 1267              &turn_context,
 1268              ResponsesStreamRequest::Sampling,
 1269          )
 1270          .await?;
 1271          turn_context.turn_timing_state.record_sampling_retry();
 1272      }
 1273  }

 1245  pub(super) fn collect_explicit_app_ids_from_skill_items(
 1246      skill_items: &[ResponseItem],
 1247      connectors: &[connectors::AppInfo],
 1248      skill_name_counts_lower: &HashMap<String, usize>,
 1249  ) -> HashSet<String> {
 1250      if skill_items.is_empty() || connectors.is_empty() {
 1251          return HashSet::new();
 1252      }
 1253
 1254      let skill_messages = skill_items
 1255          .iter()
 1256          .filter_map(|item| match item {
 1257              ResponseItem::Message { content, .. } => {
 1258                  content.iter().find_map(|content_item| match content_item {
 1259                      ContentItem::InputText { text } => Some(text.clone()),
 1260                      _ => None,
 1261                  })
 1262              }
 1263              _ => None,
 1264          })
 1265          .collect::<Vec<String>>();
 1266      if skill_messages.is_empty() {
 1267          return HashSet::new();
 1268      }
 1269
 1270      let mentions = collect_tool_mentions_from_messages(&skill_messages);
 1271      let mention_names_lower = mentions
 1272          .plain_names
 1273          .iter()
 1274          .map(|name| name.to_ascii_lowercase())
 1275          .collect::<HashSet<String>>();
 1276      let mut connector_ids = mentions
 1277          .paths
 1278          .iter()
 1279          .filter(|path| tool_kind_for_path(path) == ToolMentionKind::App)
 1280          .filter_map(|path| app_id_from_path(path).map(str::to_string))
 1281          .collect::<HashSet<String>>();
 1282
 1283      let connector_slug_counts = build_connector_slug_counts(connectors);
 1284      for connector in connectors {
 1285          let slug = codex_connectors::metadata::connector_mention_slug(connector);
 1286          let connector_count = connector_slug_counts.get(&slug).copied().unwrap_or(0);
 1287          let skill_count = skill_name_counts_lower.get(&slug).copied().unwrap_or(0);
 1288          if connector_count == 1 && skill_count == 0 && mention_names_lower.contains(&slug) {
 1289              connector_ids.insert(connector.id.clone());
 1290          }
 1291      }
 1292
 1293      connector_ids
 1294  }

04
L1facttools

04 · 工具有统一 typed runtime 契约，hooks、观测和流式参数 diff 都是一级能力

源码事实

CoreToolRuntime 扩展 ToolExecutor，统一声明 payload 匹配、取消清理、telemetry tags、pre/post hook payload、hook 输入改写和参数 diff consumer。

白话解释

每个新工具不只是写一个 execute 函数，还要说明如何取消、怎么记日志、钩子看什么、参数流到一半时如何展示。

对自研 Harness 的含义

扩展成本高于轻量 Agent，但跨工具的安全和观测语义更一致。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 48–97
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 98–149
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

codex-rs/core/src/tools/registry.rs:48–149固定提交 ↗
   48  /// Typed runtime contract for locally executed tools.
   49  ///
   50  /// Implementers provide the shared `ToolExecutor` behavior plus optional
   51  /// core-owned metadata for hooks, telemetry, tool search, and argument diffs.
   52  pub(crate) trait CoreToolRuntime: ToolExecutor<ToolInvocation> {
   53      fn matches_kind(&self, payload: &ToolPayload) -> bool {
   54          matches!(
   55              payload,
   56              ToolPayload::Function { .. } | ToolPayload::ToolSearch { .. }
   57          )
   58      }
   59
   60      /// Whether cancellation should let the handler finish teardown before the
   61      /// host returns an aborted tool response.
   62      fn waits_for_runtime_cancellation(&self) -> bool {
   63          false
   64      }
   65
   66      fn telemetry_tags<'a>(
   67          &'a self,
   68          _invocation: &'a ToolInvocation,
   69      ) -> BoxFuture<'a, ToolTelemetryTags> {
   70          Box::pin(async { Vec::new() })
   71      }
   72
   73      fn post_tool_use_payload(
   74          &self,
   75          invocation: &ToolInvocation,
   76          result: &dyn ToolOutput,
   77      ) -> Option<PostToolUsePayload> {
   78          let ToolPayload::Function { arguments } = &invocation.payload else {
   79              return None;
   80          };
   81
   82          Some(PostToolUsePayload {
   83              tool_name: function_hook_tool_name(invocation),
   84              tool_use_id: result.post_tool_use_id(&invocation.call_id),
   85              tool_input: result
   86                  .post_tool_use_input(&invocation.payload)
   87                  .unwrap_or_else(|| function_hook_tool_input(arguments)),
   88              tool_response: result
   89                  .post_tool_use_response(&invocation.call_id, &invocation.payload)
   90                  .or_else(|| {
   91                      // Most function tools can expose their model-facing output
   92                      // as the hook response. Outputs with a more stable hook
   93                      // contract should override post_tool_use_response above.
   94                      let ResponseInputItem::FunctionCallOutput {
   95                          output: FunctionCallOutputPayload { body, .. },
   96                          ..
   97                      } = result.to_response_item(&invocation.call_id, &invocation.payload)
   98                      else {
   99                          return None;
  100                      };
  101
  102                      serde_json::to_value(body).ok()
  103                  })?,
  104          })
  105      }
  106
  107      fn pre_tool_use_payload(&self, invocation: &ToolInvocation) -> Option<PreToolUsePayload> {
  108          let ToolPayload::Function { arguments } = &invocation.payload else {
  109              return None;
  110          };
  111
  112          Some(PreToolUsePayload {
  113              tool_name: function_hook_tool_name(invocation),
  114              tool_input: function_hook_tool_input(arguments),
  115          })
  116      }
  117
  118      /// Rebuilds a tool invocation from hook-facing `tool_input`.
  119      ///
  120      /// Tools that opt into input-rewriting hooks should invert the same stable
  121      /// hook contract they expose from `pre_tool_use_payload`.
  122      fn with_updated_hook_input(
  123          &self,
  124          invocation: ToolInvocation,
  125          updated_input: Value,
  126      ) -> Result<ToolInvocation, FunctionCallError> {
  127          let ToolPayload::Function { .. } = &invocation.payload else {
  128              return Err(FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(
  129                  "hook input rewrite received unsupported function tool payload".to_string(),
  130              ));
  131          };
  132
  133          let arguments = serde_json::to_string(&updated_input).map_err(|err| {
  134              FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(format!(
  135                  "failed to serialize rewritten {} arguments: {err}",
  136                  flat_tool_name(&invocation.tool_name)
  137              ))
  138          })?;
  139          Ok(ToolInvocation {
  140              payload: ToolPayload::Function { arguments },
  141              ..invocation
  142          })
  143      }
  144
  145      /// Creates an optional consumer for streamed tool argument diffs.
  146      fn create_diff_consumer(&self) -> Option<Box<dyn ToolArgumentDiffConsumer>> {
  147          None
  148      }
  149  }
  150
  151  /// Consumes streamed argument diffs for a tool call and emits protocol events
  152  /// derived from partial tool input.
  153  pub(crate) trait ToolArgumentDiffConsumer: Send {
  154      /// Consume the next argument diff for a tool call.
  155      fn consume_diff(&mut self, turn: &TurnContext, call_id: String, diff: &str)
  156      -> Option<EventMsg>;
  157
  158      /// Finish consuming argument diffs before the tool call completes.
  159      fn finish(&mut self) -> Result<Option<EventMsg>, FunctionCallError> {
  160          Ok(None)
  161      }
  162  }

   49  /// Typed runtime contract for locally executed tools.
   50  ///
   51  /// Implementers provide the shared `ToolExecutor` behavior plus optional
   52  /// core-owned metadata for hooks, telemetry, tool search, and argument diffs.
   53  pub(crate) trait CoreToolRuntime: ToolExecutor<ToolInvocation> {
   54      /// Returns a shared spec when both the spec and search metadata are immutable.
   55      fn immutable_spec(&self) -> Option<&Arc<ToolSpec>> {
   56          None
   57      }
   58
   59      /// Returns lazily cached Code Mode definitions owned by this runtime.
   60      fn cached_code_mode_definitions(&self) -> Option<&[codex_code_mode::ToolDefinition]> {
   61          None
   62      }
   63
   64      /// Returns a readiness wait for this exact tool before taking the execution gate.
   65      fn wait_until_ready<'a>(&'a self, _session: &'a Arc<Session>) -> Option<BoxFuture<'a, ()>> {
   66          None
   67      }
   68
   69      /// Returns the owning server only for MCP-backed tool runtimes.
   70      fn mcp_server_name(&self) -> Option<&str> {
   71          None
   72      }
   73
   74      fn matches_kind(&self, payload: &ToolPayload) -> bool {
   75          matches!(
   76              payload,
   77              ToolPayload::Function { .. } | ToolPayload::ToolSearch { .. }
   78          )
   79      }
   80
   81      /// Whether cancellation should let the handler finish teardown before the
   82      /// host returns an aborted tool response.
   83      fn waits_for_runtime_cancellation(&self) -> bool {
   84          false
   85      }
   86
   87      fn telemetry_tags(&self, _invocation: &ToolInvocation) -> ToolTelemetryTags {
   88          Vec::new()
   89      }
   90
   91      fn post_tool_use_payload(
   92          &self,
   93          invocation: &ToolInvocation,
   94          result: &dyn ToolOutput,
   95      ) -> Option<PostToolUsePayload> {
   96          let ToolPayload::Function { arguments } = &invocation.payload else {
   97              return None;
   98          };
   99
  100          Some(PostToolUsePayload {
  101              tool_name: function_hook_tool_name(invocation),
  102              tool_use_id: result.post_tool_use_id(&invocation.call_id),
  103              tool_input: result
  104                  .post_tool_use_input(&invocation.payload)
  105                  .unwrap_or_else(|| function_hook_tool_input(arguments)),
  106              tool_response: result
  107                  .post_tool_use_response(&invocation.call_id, &invocation.payload)
  108                  .or_else(|| {
  109                      // Most function tools can expose their model-facing output
  110                      // as the hook response. Outputs with a more stable hook
  111                      // contract should override post_tool_use_response above.
  112                      let ResponseInputItem::FunctionCallOutput {
  113                          output: FunctionCallOutputPayload { body, .. },
  114                          ..
  115                      } = result.to_response_item(&invocation.call_id, &invocation.payload)
  116                      else {
  117                          return None;
  118                      };
  119
  120                      serde_json::to_value(body).ok()
  121                  })?,
  122          })
  123      }
  124
  125      fn pre_tool_use_payload(&self, invocation: &ToolInvocation) -> Option<PreToolUsePayload> {
  126          let ToolPayload::Function { arguments } = &invocation.payload else {
  127              return None;
  128          };
  129
  130          Some(PreToolUsePayload {
  131              tool_name: function_hook_tool_name(invocation),
  132              tool_input: function_hook_tool_input(arguments),
  133          })
  134      }
  135
  136      /// Rebuilds a tool invocation from hook-facing `tool_input`.
  137      ///
  138      /// Tools that opt into input-rewriting hooks should invert the same stable
  139      /// hook contract they expose from `pre_tool_use_payload`.
  140      fn with_updated_hook_input(
  141          &self,
  142          invocation: ToolInvocation,
  143          updated_input: Value,
  144      ) -> Result<ToolInvocation, FunctionCallError> {
  145          let ToolPayload::Function { .. } = &invocation.payload else {
  146              return Err(FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(
  147                  "hook input rewrite received unsupported function tool payload".to_string(),
  148              ));
  149          };
  150
  151          let arguments = serde_json::to_string(&updated_input).map_err(|err| {
  152              FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(format!(
  153                  "failed to serialize rewritten {} arguments: {err}",
  154                  flat_tool_name(&invocation.tool_name)
  155              ))
  156          })?;
  157          Ok(ToolInvocation {
  158              payload: ToolPayload::Function { arguments },
  159              ..invocation
  160          })
  161      }
  162
  163      /// Creates an optional consumer for streamed tool argument diffs.
  164      fn create_diff_consumer(&self) -> Option<Box<dyn ToolArgumentDiffConsumer>> {
  165          None
  166      }
  167  }
  168
  169  /// Consumes streamed argument diffs for a tool call and emits protocol events
  170  /// derived from partial tool input.
  171  pub(crate) trait ToolArgumentDiffConsumer: Send {
  172      /// Consume the next argument diff for a tool call.
  173      fn consume_diff(&mut self, turn: &TurnContext, call_id: String, diff: &str)
  174      -> Option<EventMsg>;
  175
  176      /// Finish consuming argument diffs before the tool call completes.
  177      fn finish(&mut self) -> Result<Option<EventMsg>, FunctionCallError> {
  178          Ok(None)
  179      }
  180  }
  181
  182  pub(crate) struct AnyToolResult {
  183      pub(crate) call_id: String,
  184      pub(crate) payload: ToolPayload,
  185      pub(crate) result: Box<dyn ToolOutput>,
  186      pub(crate) post_tool_use_payload: Option<PostToolUsePayload>,
  187  }

  190      pub(crate) fn into_response(self) -> ResponseInputItem {
  191          let Self {
  192              call_id,
  193              payload,
  194              result,
  195              ..
  196          } = self;
  197          result.to_response_item(&call_id, &payload)
  198      }
  199
  200      pub(crate) fn code_mode_result(self) -> serde_json::Value {
  201          let Self {
  202              payload, result, ..
  203          } = self;
  204          result.code_mode_result(&payload)
  205      }
  206  }

  208  struct PostToolUseFeedbackOutput {
  209      original: Box<dyn ToolOutput>,
  210      model_visible: FunctionToolOutput,
  211  }

  213  impl ToolOutput for PostToolUseFeedbackOutput {
  214      fn log_preview(&self) -> String {
  215          self.original.log_preview()
  216      }

  218      fn success_for_logging(&self) -> bool {
  219          self.original.success_for_logging()

05
L1facttools

05 · 并发工具用读写锁实现“并行组 + 全局排他工具”

源码事实

ToolCallRuntime 对支持并发的 handler 取读锁，不支持并发的取写锁；取消时按 handler 语义等待清理或 abort，并输出结构化 aborted result 与分段时延。

白话解释

能并行的工具像多人同时读资料；危险或有状态的工具拿独占钥匙，等所有并行动作结束后自己运行。

对自研 Harness 的含义

不是简单 Promise.all，避免非并行工具与其他调用交叉污染环境。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 74–108
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 109–145
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   80          let error_call = call.clone();
   81          let future =
   82              self.handle_tool_call_with_source(call, ToolCallSource::Direct, cancellation_token);
   83          async move {
   84              match future.await {
   85                  Ok(response) => Ok(response.into_response()),
   86                  Err(FunctionCallError::Fatal(message)) => Err(CodexErr::Fatal(message)),
   87                  Err(other) => Ok(Self::failure_response(error_call, other)),
   88              }

   92
   93      #[instrument(level = "trace", skip_all)]
   94      pub(crate) fn handle_tool_call_with_source(
   95          self,
   96          call: ToolCall,
   97          source: ToolCallSource,
   98          cancellation_token: CancellationToken,
   99      ) -> impl std::future::Future<Output = Result<AnyToolResult, FunctionCallError>> {
  100          let supports_parallel = self.router.tool_supports_parallel(&call);
  101          let router = Arc::clone(&self.router);
  102          let session = Arc::clone(&self.session);
  103          let step_context = Arc::clone(&self.step_context);
  104          let turn = Arc::clone(&step_context.turn);
  105          let tracker = Arc::clone(&self.tracker);
  106          let lock = Arc::clone(&self.parallel_execution);
  107          let invocation_cancellation_token = cancellation_token.clone();
  108          let wait_for_runtime_cancellation = self.router.tool_waits_for_runtime_cancellation(&call);
  109          let started = Instant::now();
  110          let tool_call_timing_guard =
  111              ToolCallTimingGuard::capture(started, &session.thread_id, &turn.sub_id, &call, &source);
  112          let execution_started_at = tool_call_timing_guard
  113              .as_ref()
  114              .map(|timing| Arc::clone(&timing.execution_started_at));
  115          let abort_session = Arc::clone(&session);
  116          let abort_source = source.clone();
  117          let abort_turn = Arc::clone(&turn);
  118          let terminal_outcome_reached = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
  119          let dispatch_terminal_outcome_reached = Arc::clone(&terminal_outcome_reached);
  120          let dispatch_call = call.clone();
  121
  122          let dispatch_span = trace_span!(
  123              "dispatch_tool_call_with_code_mode_result",
  124              otel.name = %call.tool_name,
  125              tool_name = %call.tool_name,
  126              call_id = call.call_id.as_str(),
  127              aborted = false,
  128          );
  129          let abort_dispatch_span = dispatch_span.clone();
  130
  131          let mut dispatch_handle: AbortOnDropHandle<Result<AnyToolResult, FunctionCallError>> =
  132              AbortOnDropHandle::new(tokio::spawn(async move {
  133                  let _guard = if supports_parallel {
  134                      Either::Left(lock.read().await)
  135                  } else {
  136                      Either::Right(lock.write().await)
  137                  };
  138                  // Admission through the parallel-execution gate marks the end
  139                  // of dispatch waiting and the start of handler execution.
  140                  if let Some(execution_started_at) = execution_started_at {
  141                      let _ = execution_started_at.set(Instant::now());
  142                  }
  143
  144                  router
  145                      .dispatch_tool_call_with_terminal_outcome(
  146                          session,
  147                          step_context,
  148                          invocation_cancellation_token,
  149                          tracker,
  150                          dispatch_call,
  151                          source,
  152                          dispatch_terminal_outcome_reached,
  153                      )
  154                      .instrument(dispatch_span.clone())
  155                      .await
  156              }));
  157
  158          async move {
  159              let _tool_call_timing_guard = tool_call_timing_guard;
  160              tokio::select! {
  161                  res = &mut dispatch_handle => res.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?,
  162                  _ = cancellation_token.cancelled() => {
  163                      if terminal_outcome_reached.load(Ordering::Acquire) || dispatch_handle.is_finished() {
  164                          dispatch_handle.await.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?
  165                      } else {
  166                          let secs = started.elapsed().as_secs_f32().max(0.1);
  167                          abort_dispatch_span.record("aborted", true);
  168                          if wait_for_runtime_cancellation {
  169                              if terminal_outcome_reached.swap(true, Ordering::AcqRel) {
  170                                  return dispatch_handle.await.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?;
  171                              }
  172                              // The abort owns the terminal outcome; await only so
  173                              // the runtime can finish process teardown.
  174                              match dispatch_handle.await {
  175                                  Ok(_) => {}
  176                                  Err(err) if err.is_cancelled() => {}
  177                                  Err(err) => return Err(Self::tool_task_join_error(err)),
  178                              }
  179                          } else {
  180                              dispatch_handle.abort();
  181                              match dispatch_handle.await {
  182                                  Ok(result) => return result,
  183                                  Err(err) if err.is_cancelled() => {}
  184                                  Err(err) => return Err(Self::tool_task_join_error(err)),
  185                              }
  186                          }
  187                          let response = Self::aborted_response(&call, secs);
  188                          notify_tool_aborted(
  189                              abort_session.as_ref(),
  190                              abort_turn.as_ref(),
  191                              call.call_id.as_str(),
  192                              &call.tool_name,
  193                              abort_source,
  194                          )
  195                          .await;
  196                          Ok(response)
  197                      }
  198                  },
  199              }
  200          }
  201          .in_current_span()
  202      }
  203  }
  204
  205  impl ToolCallRuntime {

  215                  status: "completed".to_string(),
  216                  execution: "client".to_string(),

   98          if self

  100              .turn

  102              .features

  111          }

  148                      && let Some(readiness) = tool_runtime.wait_until_ready(&session)
  149                  {

  151                  }

  155                  } else {

  157                  };

  173                      )

  175                      .await
  176              }));

  178          async move {
  179              let _tool_call_timing_guard = tool_call_timing_guard;
  180              tokio::select! {
  181                  res = &mut dispatch_handle => res.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?,
  182                  _ = cancellation_token.cancelled() => {
  183                      if terminal_outcome_reached.load(Ordering::Acquire) || dispatch_handle.is_finished() {
  184                          dispatch_handle.await.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?
  185                      } else {
  186                          let secs = started.elapsed().as_secs_f32().max(0.1);
  187                          abort_dispatch_span.record("aborted", true);
  188                          if wait_for_runtime_cancellation {
  189                              if terminal_outcome_reached.swap(true, Ordering::AcqRel) {
  190                                  return dispatch_handle.await.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?;
  191                              }
  192                              // The abort owns the terminal outcome; await only so
  193                              // the runtime can finish process teardown.
  194                              match dispatch_handle.await {
  195                                  Ok(_) => {}
  196                                  Err(err) if err.is_cancelled() => {}
  197                                  Err(err) => return Err(Self::tool_task_join_error(err)),
  198                              }
  199                          } else {
  200                              dispatch_handle.abort();
  201                              match dispatch_handle.await {
  202                                  Ok(result) => return result,
  203                                  Err(err) if err.is_cancelled() => {}
  204                                  Err(err) => return Err(Self::tool_task_join_error(err)),
  205                              }
  206                          }
  207                          let response = Self::aborted_response(&call, secs);
  208                          notify_tool_aborted(
  209                              abort_session.as_ref(),
  210                              abort_turn.as_ref(),
  211                              call.call_id.as_str(),
  212                              &call.tool_name,
  213                              abort_source,
  214                          )
  215                          .await;
  216                          Ok(response)
06
L3facttools

06 · 工具暴露与分发分离，兼容旧 shell 又不污染模型可见清单

源码事实

工具规划测试确认 unified exec 可把 exec_command/write_stdin 暴露给模型，同时保留隐藏的 legacy shell_command 仅供分发；apply_patch、web search 和动态工具也按环境、模型及 Provider capability gate。

白话解释

后台可以保留旧插座做兼容，但模型眼前只摆当前应该用的工具，避免同功能多把扳手。

对自研 Harness 的含义

迁移期兼容性强，且 prompt tool schema 保持紧凑稳定。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 637–671
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 672–708
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

07
L1factconnectors

07 · MCP 是带复用、认证、required gate 和 catalog revision 的运行时

源码事实

McpConnectionSet 同时管理 server、required servers、工具目录版本、Codex Apps cache、plugin provenance、工具命名前缀和 elicitation；连接配置/OAuth 凭据一致且存活时才复用。

白话解释

它不是每轮临时扫一遍外接工具，而是维护一套有版本、有健康状态、有认证身份的连接池。

对自研 Harness 的含义

工具清单可稳定捕获到 step snapshot，连接变化不会悄悄污染正在执行的一步。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 66–90
    入口、配置和状态初始化

  2. 91–117
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

codex-rs/codex-mcp/src/connection_manager.rs:66–117固定提交 ↗
   66  pub(crate) struct McpServerConnection {
   67      identity: Option<McpServerConnectionIdentity>,
   68      client: AsyncManagedClient,
   69  }
   70
   71  impl McpServerConnection {
   72      async fn reusable_client(
   73          &self,
   74          desired: &McpServerConnectionIdentity,
   75      ) -> Option<ManagedClient> {
   76          let current = self.identity.as_ref()?;
   77          if !current.has_same_connection_config(desired) {
   78              return None;
   79          }
   80          if !self.client.startup_complete.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
   81              return None;
   82          }
   83          let client = self.client.client().await.ok()?;
   84          if client.client.is_closed().await {
   85              return None;
   86          }
   87          let Ok(desired_credentials) = desired.oauth_credentials() else {
   88              return Some(client);
   89          };
   90          let reusable = match client.client.managed_oauth_credentials().await {
   91              Some(live_credentials) => &live_credentials == desired_credentials,
   92              None => current
   93                  .oauth_credentials()
   94                  .is_ok_and(|startup_credentials| startup_credentials == desired_credentials),
   95          };
   96          if reusable { Some(client) } else { None }
   97      }
   98
   99      pub(crate) async fn client(&self) -> Result<ManagedClient, StartupOutcomeError> {
  100          self.client.client().await
  101      }
  102
  103      async fn shutdown(&self) {
  104          self.client.shutdown().await;
  105      }
  106
  107      fn cancel_startup(&self) {
  108          if !self.client.startup_complete.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
  109              self.client.cancel_token.cancel();
  110          }
  111      }
  112  }
  113
  114  impl Drop for McpServerConnection {
  115      fn drop(&mut self) {
  116          self.client.cancel_token.cancel();
  117      }
  118  }
  119

  121  struct McpServerView {
  122      connection: Arc<McpServerConnection>,
  123      metadata: McpServerMetadata,
  124      tool_filter: ToolFilter,
  125      tool_timeout: Option<Duration>,
  126  }
  127
  128  impl McpServerView {
  129      async fn listed_tools(
  130          &self,
  131          tool_plugin_provenance: &ToolPluginProvenance,
  132      ) -> Option<Vec<ToolInfo>> {
  133          let tools = self.connection.client.listed_tools().await?;
  134          let tools = filter_tools(tools, &self.tool_filter);
  135          Some(if self.connection.client.is_codex_apps_mcp_server {
  136              prepare_codex_apps_tools_for_model(tools, tool_plugin_provenance)
  137          } else {
  138              prepare_regular_mcp_tools_for_model(tools, tool_plugin_provenance)
  139          })
  140      }
  141  }
  142

  186          let initial_permission_profile = config.permission_profile.clone();

   66  use codex_protocol::protocol::McpStartupFailure;
   67  use codex_protocol::protocol::McpStartupFailureReason;
   68  use codex_protocol::protocol::McpStartupStatus;
   69  use codex_protocol::protocol::McpStartupUpdateEvent;
   70  use codex_rmcp_client::determine_streamable_http_auth_status_from_credentials;
   71  use tokio::sync::Mutex;
   72  use tokio::sync::RwLock;
   73  use tokio::sync::watch;
   74  use tokio::task::JoinSet;
   75  use tracing::warn;
   76
   77  static LIVE_CONNECTIONS: Gauge = Gauge::new("mcp.connections.live");
   78
   79  pub(crate) struct McpServerConnection {
   80      identity: Option<McpServerConnectionIdentity>,
   81      client: AsyncManagedClient,
   82      startup_trigger: Option<watch::Sender<bool>>,
   83      _diagnostics_guard: GaugeGuard,
   84  }
   85
   86  impl McpServerConnection {
   87      async fn reusable_client(
   88          &self,
   89          desired: &McpServerConnectionIdentity,
   90      ) -> Option<ManagedClient> {
   91          let current = self.identity.as_ref()?;
   92          if !current.has_same_connection_config(desired) {
   93              return None;
   94          }
   95          if !self.client.startup_complete.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
   96              return None;
   97          }
   98          let client = self.client.client().await.ok()?;
   99          if client.client.is_closed().await {
  100              return None;
  101          }
  102          if current == desired {
  103              if matches!(desired.oauth_credentials(), Ok(None))
  104                  && tokio::time::timeout(Duration::ZERO, client.client.managed_oauth_credentials())
  105                      .await
  106                      .is_ok_and(|credentials| matches!(credentials, Some(Some(_))))
  107              {
  108                  return None;
  109              }
  110              return Some(client);
  111          }
  112          let Ok(desired_credentials) = desired.oauth_credentials() else {
  113              return Some(client);
  114          };
  115          let reusable = match client.client.managed_oauth_credentials().await {
  116              Some(live_credentials) => live_credentials.as_ref() == desired_credentials,
  117              None => current
  118                  .oauth_credentials()
  119                  .is_ok_and(|startup_credentials| startup_credentials == desired_credentials),
  120          };
  121          if reusable { Some(client) } else { None }
  122      }
  123
  124      pub(crate) async fn client(&self) -> Result<ManagedClient, StartupOutcomeError> {
  125          if let Some(startup_trigger) = &self.startup_trigger {
  126              startup_trigger.send_replace(true);
  127          }
  128          self.client.client().await
  129      }
  130
  131      async fn shutdown(&self) {
  132          self.client.shutdown().await;
  133      }
  134
  135      fn cancel_startup(&self) {
  136          if !self.startup_is_dormant() && !self.client.startup_complete.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
  137              self.client.cancel_token.cancel();
  138          }
  139      }
  140
  141      fn startup_is_dormant(&self) -> bool {
  142          self.startup_trigger
  143              .as_ref()
  144              .is_some_and(|startup_trigger| !*startup_trigger.borrow())
  145      }
  146  }
  147
  148  impl Drop for McpServerConnection {
  149      fn drop(&mut self) {
  150          self.client.cancel_token.cancel();
  151      }
  152  }
  153
  154  #[derive(Clone)]
  155  struct McpServerView {
  156      connection: Arc<McpServerConnection>,
  157      metadata: McpServerMetadata,
  158      tool_filter: ToolFilter,
  159      tool_timeout: Option<Duration>,
  160      catalog_item_limit: usize,
  161  }
  162
  163  impl McpServerView {
  164      async fn listed_tools(
  165          &self,
  166          tool_plugin_provenance: &ToolPluginProvenance,
  167      ) -> Option<Vec<ToolInfo>> {
  168          let tools = self.connection.client.listed_tools().await?;
  169          let tools = filter_tools(tools, &self.tool_filter);
  170          Some(if self.connection.client.is_codex_apps_mcp_server {
  171              prepare_codex_apps_tools_for_model(tools, tool_plugin_provenance)
  172          } else {
  173              prepare_regular_mcp_tools_for_model(tools, tool_plugin_provenance)
  174          })
  175      }
  176  }
  177
  178  /// A published view over a set of running MCP server connections.
  179  pub(crate) struct McpConnectionSet {
  180      servers: HashMap<String, McpServerView>,
  181      protocol_mode: crate::McpProtocolMode,
  182      required_servers: Vec<String>,
  183      optional_startup_deadline: OnceLock<tokio::time::Instant>,
  184      tool_catalog_revision: Arc<RwLock<u64>>,
  185      codex_apps_tools_override: RwLock<Option<Vec<ToolInfo>>>,
  186      codex_apps_refresh_lock: Mutex<()>,
  187      tool_plugin_provenance: Arc<ToolPluginProvenance>,
  188      prefix_mcp_tool_names: bool,
08
L1factconnectors

08 · 模型只能看到显式可见且能绑定到同一目录版本的 MCP 工具

源码事实

tool_catalog 过滤 UI visibility，不含 metadata 时默认可见；capture_binding 在读锁下捕获 revision、ready client、过滤后的 tools 和 prepared calls，无法精确绑定的工具被省略。

白话解释

展示给模型的工具名和真正执行它的连接必须来自同一版目录，不能拿新版菜单去点旧版厨房。

对自研 Harness 的含义

减少热刷新导致的工具错配和 TOCTOU 风险。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 34–43
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 44–55
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   34  /// Returns whether a tool may be included in model-facing tool declarations.
   35  ///
   36  /// Tools without visibility metadata remain visible. Tools with visibility
   37  /// metadata are hidden unless they explicitly include `model`.
   38  ///
   39  /// <https://github.com/modelcontextprotocol/ext-apps/blob/main/specification/2026-01-26/apps.mdx#resource-discovery>
   40  pub fn tool_is_model_visible(tool: &ToolInfo) -> bool {
   41      let Some(visibility) = tool
   42          .tool
   43          .meta
   44          .as_deref()
   45          .and_then(|meta| meta.get(MCP_UI_META_KEY))
   46          .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object)
   47          .and_then(|ui| ui.get(MCP_UI_VISIBILITY_META_KEY))
   48          .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array)
   49      else {
   50          return true;
   51      };
   52      visibility
   53          .iter()
   54          .any(|target| target.as_str() == Some(MCP_UI_MODEL_VISIBILITY))
   55  }

   57  impl McpConnectionSet {
   58      /// Returns all tools with model-visible names normalized.
   59      #[instrument(level = "trace", skip_all, fields(mcp_server_count = self.servers.len()))]
   60      pub async fn list_all_tools(&self) -> Vec<ToolInfo> {
   61          let mut tools = Vec::new();
   62          let mut available_server_count = 0;
   63          let mut unavailable_server_count = 0;
   64          for (server_name, view) in &self.servers {
   65              view.connection.client.reconnect_failed_startup().await;
   66              let has_cached_tools = view.connection.client.has_cached_tools();
   67              let startup_complete = view
   68                  .connection
   69                  .client
   70                  .startup_complete
   71                  .load(Ordering::Acquire);
   72              let catalog_override = if server_name == CODEX_APPS_MCP_SERVER_NAME {
   73                  self.codex_apps_tools_override.read().await.clone()
   74              } else {
   75                  None
   76              };
   77              let Some(server_tools) = async {
   78                  match catalog_override {
   79                      Some(tools) => {
   80                          let tools = filter_tools(tools, &view.tool_filter);
   81                          Some(prepare_codex_apps_tools_for_model(
   82                              tools,
   83                              &self.tool_plugin_provenance,
   84                          ))
   85                      }
   86                      None => view.listed_tools(&self.tool_plugin_provenance).await,
   87                  }
   88              }
   89              .instrument(trace_span!(
   90                  "list_tools_for_server",
   91                  server_name = %server_name,
   92                  has_cached_tools,
   93                  startup_complete
   94              ))
   95              .await
   96              else {
   97                  unavailable_server_count += 1;
   98                  trace!(
   99                      server_name = %server_name,
  100                      has_cached_tools,
  101                      startup_complete,
  102                      "MCP server tools unavailable while building tool list"
  103                  );
  104                  continue;
  105              };
  106              available_server_count += 1;
  107              tools.extend(
  108                  server_tools
  109                      .into_iter()
  110                      .map(|tool| Self::with_server_metadata(tool, &view.metadata)),
  111              );
  112          }
  113          let tools = normalize_tools_for_model_with_prefix(
  114              tools,
  115              self.prefix_mcp_tool_names,
  116              &self.non_prefixed_mcp_tool_servers,
  117          );
  118          trace!(
  119              available_server_count,
  120              unavailable_server_count,
  121              tool_count = tools.len(),
  122              "built MCP tool list"
  123          );
  124          tools
  125      }

   61      pub(crate) async fn stable_catalog_revision(&self) -> Option<u64> {
   62          for (server_name, view) in &self.servers {
   63              if !view
   64                  .connection
   65                  .client
   66                  .startup_complete
   67                  .load(Ordering::Acquire)
   68              {
   69                  return None;
   70              }

  298  fn create_approval_parameters(
  299      exec_permission_approvals_enabled: bool,
  300  ) -> BTreeMap<String, JsonSchema> {
  301      let mut sandbox_permission_values = vec![json!("use_default")];

  304      }
  305      sandbox_permission_values.push(json!("require_escalated"));

  102  pub fn create_spawn_agent_tool_v2(options: SpawnAgentToolOptions) -> ToolSpec {
  103      let available_models_description = options.expose_spawn_agent_model_overrides.then(|| {
  104          spawn_agent_models_description(&options.available_models, options.multi_agent_version)
  105      });
  106      let inherited_model_guidance = (options.expose_spawn_agent_model_overrides
  107          && !options.hide_agent_type_model_reasoning)
  108          .then_some(SPAWN_AGENT_INHERITED_MODEL_GUIDANCE);
  109      let mut properties = spawn_agent_common_properties_v2(&options.agent_type_description);
  110      if !options.expose_agent_type {
  111          properties.remove("agent_type");
  112      }
  113      if options.hide_agent_type_model_reasoning {
  114          properties.remove("service_tier");
  115      }
  116      if !options.expose_spawn_agent_model_overrides {
  117          properties.remove("model");
  118          properties.remove("reasoning_effort");
  119      }
  120      properties.insert(
  121          "task_name".to_string(),
  122          JsonSchema::string(Some(
  123              "Task name for the new agent. Use lowercase letters, digits, and underscores."
  124                  .to_string(),
  125          )),
  126      );
  127
  128      ToolSpec::Function(ResponsesApiTool {
  129          name: "spawn_agent".to_string(),
  130          description: spawn_agent_tool_description_v2(
  131              available_models_description.as_deref(),
  132              inherited_model_guidance,
  133              options.usage_hint_text,
  134          ),
  135          strict: false,
  136          defer_loading: None,
  137          parameters: JsonSchema::object(
  138              properties,
  139              Some(vec!["task_name".to_string(), "message".to_string()]),
  140              Some(false.into()),
  141          ),
  142          output_schema: Some(spawn_agent_output_schema_v2(
  143              options.hide_agent_type_model_reasoning,
  144          )),
  145      })
  146  }

  186      }

  188          options.exec_permission_approvals_enabled,
  189      ));

  193              r#"Runs a Powershell command (Windows) and returns its output.

  924  impl FeatureToggles {
  925      fn to_overrides(&self) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<String>> {
  926          let mut v = Vec::new();
  927          for feature in &self.enable {
  928              Self::validate_feature(feature)?;
  929              v.push(format!("features.{feature}=true"));
  930          }
  931          for feature in &self.disable {
  932              Self::validate_feature(feature)?;
  933              v.push(format!("features.{feature}=false"));
  934          }
  935          Ok(v)
  936      }
  937
  938      fn validate_feature(feature: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
  939          if is_known_feature_key(feature) {
  940              Ok(())
  941          } else {
  942              anyhow::bail!("Unknown feature flag: {feature}")
  943          }
  944      }
  945  }
  946

  796                  // On Windows, run via cmd.exe so .CMD/.BAT are correctly resolved (PATHEXT semantics).

  811                  .args(&normalized_args)
  812                  .status()?
  813          }
  814      };

  924  impl FeatureToggles {
  925      fn to_overrides(&self) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<String>> {
  926          let mut v = Vec::new();
  927          for feature in &self.enable {
  928              Self::validate_feature(feature)?;
  929              v.push(format!("features.{feature}=true"));
  930          }
  931          for feature in &self.disable {
  932              Self::validate_feature(feature)?;
  933              v.push(format!("features.{feature}=false"));
  934          }
  935          Ok(v)
  936      }
  937
  938      fn validate_feature(feature: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
  939          if is_known_feature_key(feature) {
  940              Ok(())
  941          } else {
  942              anyhow::bail!("Unknown feature flag: {feature}")
  943          }
  944      }
  945  }
  946
  947  #[derive(Debug, Parser)]
  948  struct FeaturesCli {
  949      #[command(subcommand)]
  950      sub: FeaturesSubcommand,
  951  }
  952
  953  #[derive(Debug, Parser)]
  954  enum FeaturesSubcommand {
  955      /// List known features with their stage and effective state.
  956      List,
  957      /// Enable a feature in config.toml.
  958      Enable(FeatureSetArgs),
  959      /// Disable a feature in config.toml.
  960      Disable(FeatureSetArgs),
  961  }
  962
  963  #[derive(Debug, Parser)]
  964  struct FeatureSetArgs {
  965      /// Feature key to update (for example: unified_exec).
  966      feature: String,
  967  }
  968
  969  fn stage_str(stage: Stage) -> &'static str {
  970      match stage {
  971          Stage::UnderDevelopment => "under development",
  972          Stage::Experimental { .. } => "experimental",
  973          Stage::Stable => "stable",
  974          Stage::Deprecated => "deprecated",
  975          Stage::Removed => "removed",
  976      }
  977  }

  101      marketplace_name: Option<String>,

  104      #[arg(long = "json")]
  105      json: bool,
  106  }
  107
  108  #[derive(Debug, Parser)]
  109  #[command(
  110      bin_name = "codex plugin marketplace remove",
  111      after_help = "Example:\n  codex plugin marketplace remove debug"
  112  )]
  113  struct RemoveMarketplaceArgs {
  114      /// Configured marketplace name to remove.
  115      #[arg(value_name = "MARKETPLACE_NAME")]
  116      marketplace_name: String,
  117
  118      /// Output remove result as JSON.
  119      #[arg(long = "json")]
  120      json: bool,
  121  }
  122
  123  impl MarketplaceCli {
  124      pub async fn run(self) -> Result<()> {
  125          let MarketplaceCli {
  126              config_overrides,
  127              subcommand,
  128          } = self;
  129
  130          let overrides = config_overrides
  131              .parse_overrides()
  132              .map_err(anyhow::Error::msg)?;
  133
  134          match subcommand {
  135              MarketplaceSubcommand::Add(args) => run_add(overrides, args).await?,
  136              MarketplaceSubcommand::List(args) => run_list(overrides, args).await?,
  137              MarketplaceSubcommand::Upgrade(args) => run_upgrade(overrides, args).await?,
  138              MarketplaceSubcommand::Remove(args) => run_remove(args).await?,
  139          }
  140
  141          Ok(())
  142      }
  143  }
  144
  145  async fn run_add(overrides: Vec<(String, toml::Value)>, args: AddMarketplaceArgs) -> Result<()> {
  146      let AddMarketplaceArgs {
  147          source,
  148          ref_name,
  149          sparse_paths,
  150          json,
  151      } = args;
  152
  153      let config = Config::load_with_cli_overrides(overrides)
  154          .await
  155          .context("failed to load configuration")?;
  156      let outcome = add_marketplace(
  157          config.codex_home.to_path_buf(),

pair

CLI 子命令 · clap Subcommand variant · codex-rs/cli/src/remote_control_cmd.rs:61
源码 ↗

/ Create and print a short-lived manual pairing code.

pair→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
   56
   57      /// Stop the app-server daemon.
   58      Stop,
   59
   60      /// Create and print a short-lived manual pairing code.
   61      Pair,
   62  }
   63
   64  pub(crate) async fn run(
   65      command: RemoteControlCommand,
   66      arg0_paths: Arg0DispatchPaths,
   67      root_config_overrides: CliConfigOverrides,
   68  ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
   69      match command.subcommand {
   70          None => {
   71              print_remote_control_progress(
   72                  command.json,
   73                  "Starting app-server with remote control enabled...",
   74              )?;
   75              run_foreground_remote_control(command.json, arg0_paths, root_config_overrides).await?;
   76          }
   77          Some(RemoteControlSubcommand::Start) => {
   78              print_remote_control_progress(
   79                  command.json,
   80                  "Starting app-server daemon with remote control enabled...",
   81              )?;
   82              let output = codex_app_server_daemon::ensure_remote_control_ready().await?;
   83              print_remote_control_start_output(&output, command.json)?;
   84          }
   85          Some(RemoteControlSubcommand::Stop) => {
   86              print_remote_control_progress(command.json, "Stopping remote control...")?;
   87              let output = codex_app_server_daemon::run(AppServerLifecycleCommand::Stop).await?;
   88              print_remote_control_stop_output(&output, command.json)?;
   89          }
   90          Some(RemoteControlSubcommand::Pair) => {
   91              let output = codex_app_server_daemon::start_remote_control_pairing().await?;
   92              print_remote_control_pairing_output(&output, command.json)?;
   93          }
   94      }
   95      Ok(())
   96  }
   97
   98  fn print_remote_control_progress(json: bool, message: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
   99      if json {
  100          return Ok(());
  101      }
  102
  103      println!("{message}");
  104      std::io::stdout()
  105          .flush()
  106          .context("failed to flush remote-control progress message")?;
  107      Ok(())
  108  }
  109
  110  async fn run_foreground_remote_control(
  111      json: bool,
  112      arg0_paths: Arg0DispatchPaths,
  113      root_config_overrides: CliConfigOverrides,
  114  ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
  115      let socket_dir = tempfile::Builder::new()
  116          .prefix("codex-rc-")
  117          .tempdir_in("/tmp")
  118          .or_else(|_| tempfile::tempdir())
  119          .context("failed to create private app-server socket directory")?;
  120      let socket_path = socket_dir.path().join("rc.sock");

  726  fn format_exit_messages(exit_info: AppExitInfo, color_enabled: bool) -> Vec<String> {
  727      let is_fatal = matches!(&exit_info.exit_reason, ExitReason::Fatal(_));
  728      let AppExitInfo {
  729          token_usage,
  730          thread_id: conversation_id,
  731          resume_hint,
  732          ..
  733      } = exit_info;
  734
  735      let mut lines = Vec::new();
  736      if !token_usage.is_zero() {
  737          lines.push(token_usage.to_string());
  738      }
  739
  740      if let Some(resume_cmd) = resume_hint {
  741          let command = if color_enabled {
  742              resume_cmd.cyan().to_string()
  743          } else {
  744              resume_cmd
  745          };
  746          lines.push(format!("To continue this session, run {command}"));
  747      } else if is_fatal && let Some(conversation_id) = conversation_id {

  699      #[arg(short = 'o', long = "out", value_name = "DIR")]
  700      out_dir: PathBuf,
  701
  702      /// Include experimental methods and fields in the generated output
  703      #[arg(long = "experimental", default_value_t = false)]
  704      experimental: bool,
  705  }
  706
  707  #[derive(Debug, Args)]
  708  struct GenerateInternalJsonSchemaCommand {
  709      /// Output directory where internal JSON Schema artifacts will be written
  710      #[arg(short = 'o', long = "out", value_name = "DIR")]
  711      out_dir: PathBuf,
  712  }
  713
  714  #[derive(Debug, Parser)]
  715  struct StdioToUdsCommand {
  716      /// Path to the Unix domain socket to connect to.
  717      #[arg(value_name = "SOCKET_PATH", value_parser = parse_socket_path)]
  718      socket_path: AbsolutePathBuf,
  719  }

四问：从代码追到执行语义

COMMANDS · 四个实现问题
ENTRY

入口 / 注册

先定位谁创建这次运行、注册了哪些能力，以及输入怎样进入状态。

run_turn 在采样前处理压缩、skills/plugins、session/input hooks 和待处理输入；随后为一个 step 捕获 StepContext，并用同一视图构造上下文、工具与模型请求。
codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs:153–274 ↗
STATE

状态 / 投影

再找会话、上下文、队列或 projection 的所有权；状态不是 UI 文案。

try_run_sampling_request 消费流式 ResponseEvent；完整 tool item 到达后进入 FuturesOrdered，工具参数 diff 可边流边展示；若 commentary/reasoning 期间 mailbox 有消息，则返回 needs_follow_up。
codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs:2034–2168 ↗
EFFECT

操作 / 事务

把模型输出、工具执行、权限检查和外部副作用拆开，确认有没有回执。

run_sampling_request 在一个 turn 内复用 ModelClientSession；上下文超限和 usage limit 直接上抛，其他可重试错误才按 Provider 的 stream retry 上限重试。
codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs:1176–1273 ↗
RECOVERY

失败 / 恢复

最后追踪重试、压缩、取消、回滚、持久化或人工接管出口。

CoreToolRuntime 扩展 ToolExecutor，统一声明 payload 匹配、取消清理、telemetry tags、pre/post hook payload、hook 输入改写和参数 diff consumer。

    1  //! Shared helpers for filtering and matching built-in and model service-tier slash commands.
    2  //!
    3  //! The same sandbox- and feature-gating rules are used by both the composer
    4  //! and the command popup. Centralizing them here keeps those call sites small
    5  //! and ensures they stay in sync.
    6  use std::str::FromStr;
    7
    8  use codex_utils_fuzzy_match::fuzzy_match;
    9
   10  use crate::slash_command::SlashCommand;
   11  use crate::slash_command::built_in_slash_commands;
   12
   13  #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
   14  pub(crate) struct ServiceTierCommand {
   15      pub(crate) id: String,
   16      pub(crate) name: String,
   17      pub(crate) description: String,
   18  }
   19
   20  #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
   21  pub(crate) enum SlashCommandItem {
   22      Builtin(SlashCommand),
   23      ServiceTier(ServiceTierCommand),
   24  }
   25
   26  impl SlashCommandItem {
   27      pub(crate) fn command(&self) -> &str {
   28          match self {
   29              Self::Builtin(cmd) => cmd.command(),
   30              Self::ServiceTier(command) => &command.name,
   31          }
   32      }

    1  //! Slash-command dispatch and local-recall handoff for `ChatWidget`.
    2  //!
    3  //! `ChatComposer` parses slash input and stages recognized command text for local
    4  //! Up-arrow recall before returning an input result. This module owns the app-level
    5  //! dispatch step and records the staged entry once the command has been handled, so
    6  //! slash-command recall follows the same submitted-input rule as ordinary text.
    7
    8  use super::*;
